([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/missing-data-imputation-bpca))
- **missing-data-imputation-genomic-variants**: Use when constructing a variant pathogenicity training set (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/missing-data-imputation-genomic-variants))
- **missing-data-imputer**: Impute missing values with simple strategies (mean, median, mode), KNN imputation, iterative imputation modeling each feature as a function of the others, or MICE for multiple imputation. Use this skill when fitting imputers on the TRAIN split only to prevent leakage and documenting which columns were imputed with which strategy. ([HossamBalaha/SkillStack](https://github.com/HossamBalaha/SkillStack/tree/HEAD/data-engineering/missing-data-imputer))
- **missing-data-likelihood-derivation**: Use when fitting a LUCID integrative clustering model (G exposures, Z omics, Y outcome) where a subset of observations has list-wise missing omics data, and you need to modify EM responsibilities and M-step updates for the missing block. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-multi-omics/tree/HEAD/collections/multi-omics/v1/leaves/missing-data-likelihood-derivation))
- **missing-data-mechanism-assessment**: Use when when you have a filtered metabolite abundance matrix with remaining missing values after feature-level filtering (e.g., removal of metabolites with &gt;80% missingness), and you need to decide whether missingness is concentrated in specific features or sample pairs, or distributed randomly. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-data-mechanism-assessment))
- **missing-data-mechanism-specification**: Use when when you have a metabolomics abundance table with missing values and need to decide which imputation method to apply, or when designing a simulation to evaluate imputation performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-data-mechanism-specification) · 3 copies)
- **missing-data-simulation-and-recovery**: Use when you have metabolomics peak intensity data with pathway annotations and want to benchmark whether a pathway scoring method (PALS, ORA, GSEA) degrades gracefully under conditions of missing peaks and measurement noise. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-data-simulation-and-recovery))
- **missing-data-simulation-in-genomic-windows**: Use when evaluating robustness of a 101-base-window RNA editing classifier to missing RNAseq coverage — e.g. quantifying FP/FN rates after randomly swapping RNAseq nucleotide frequencies for genomic reference frequencies in &gt;30% of flanking positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/missing-data-simulation-in-genomic-windows))
- **missing-data-simulation-in-omics**: Use when when comparing the robustness of multiple pathway ranking methods (e.g., PLAGE, ORA, GSEA) on metabolomics or other omics data, and you need to establish which method is least sensitive to peak dropout, instrumental noise, or annotation uncertainty. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/missing-data-simulation-in-omics) · 2 copies)
- **missing-data-strategy-checklist**: Generate a draft checklist for planning missing data handling in a study. Drafts for research and education; a qualified human must review before use. ([salikkhann/openmedskills](https://github.com/salikkhann/openmedskills/tree/HEAD/dist/agent-skills/missing-data-strategy-checklist) · ★5)
- **missing-data-tolerant-meta-analysis-computation**: Use when combining published fold-changes and P-values across metabolomics studies that report only overall statistics (no raw data, error, or variance), with incomplete/missing values per compound. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/missing-value-imputation-feature-table))
- **missing-value-imputation-for-column-metadata**: Use when when preparing raw HPLC column parameter arrays for featurization into feature vectors for retention time prediction models. Specifically apply this skill when column metadata contains empty strings (indicating missing diameter or pH values) or non-standard string encodings (e.g., '2. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-for-column-metadata) · 2 copies)
- **missing-value-imputation-for-omics**: Use when your metabolomic peak table contains missing values (e.g., undetected metabolites below instrument sensitivity or sparse measurements) and you are preparing multi-class or time-course data for statistical analysis, differential profiling, or biomarker discovery. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-for-omics))
- **missing-value-imputation-for-pca**: Use when your metabolomic dataset contains missing values (common in untargeted or targeted mass spectrometry data) and you need to perform PCA for outlier detection at multiple standard deviation thresholds (e.g., 3 SD, 4 SD, 5 SD) on principal component scores. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-for-pca))
- **missing-value-imputation-in-metabolomics**: Use when after feature extraction and quality control filtering (blank masking, sample dropping, normalization) have been applied, but before statistical analysis or machine learning. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-in-metabolomics) · 3 copies)
- **missing-value-imputation-in-quantification-tables**: Use when after feature alignment across multiple LC-MS/MS runs, when the unified feature list contains zeros or nulls for specific feature–sample pairs because peaks were not detected in those individual runs, but the feature was detected in other samples in the cohort. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-in-quantification-tables) · 3 copies)
- **missing-value-imputation-knn**: Use when a metabolomics featuredata matrix (log-transformed peak intensities) still contains missing values that must be filled before normalization functions such as NormScaling can be run. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/missing-value-imputation-knn))
- **missing-value-imputation-metabolomics**: Use when your raw metabolomics dataset contains missing values (NAs) in metabolite columns after loading and you have already identified and removed metabolite columns with &gt;10% missing data prevalence. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-metabolomics))
- **missing-value-imputation-method-comparison**: Use when a cleaned LC-MS feature table (mass_dataset object) still contains missing values and you need to assess whether the choice of imputation method changes the differential-metabolite and pathway-enrichment conclusions between study groups. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/missing-value-imputation-method-comparison))
- **missing-value-imputation-method-dispatch**: Use when you have a metabolomics data matrix with missing-not-at-random (MNAR) left-censored values and need to apply multiple imputation methods to the same data in a standardized way, compare their outputs, or integrate the imputation step into a larger pre-processing pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-method-dispatch))
- **missing-value-imputation-omics**: Use when an rROMA (or similar PCA-based gene set activity) analysis is run on an expression matrix that contains missing values, and PC1-based module scores require a complete global matrix. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-transcriptomics/tree/HEAD/collections/transcriptomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-omics))
- **missing-value-imputation-strategy**: Use when your feature intensity table (samples × compounds) contains NA values and you intend to apply log transformation or scaling-based normalization. Specifically, apply this skill when the transf_data function is invoked with missing_replace=TRUE, before any log transformation or scaling step. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-strategy))
- **missing-value-imputation-strategy-selection**: Use when after mark_nas() has replaced non-NA missing-value codes (e.g., 0, 1) with R's NA in the exprs matrix of a MetaboSet object, and you need to decide whether to apply random forest imputation, simple imputation strategies, or defer imputation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-strategy-selection) · 2 copies)
- **missing-value-imputation-with-k-nearest-neighbour**: Use when you have log-transformed metabolomics data in SummarizedExperiment format organized as multiple batches, have already filtered metabolites with &gt;50% missing values per batch and retained only those quantified across all batches via intersect method, and now need to impute remaining missing. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-with-k-nearest-neighbour))
- **missing-value-imputation-with-na**: Use when you are implementing a custom MsBackend subclass for the Spectra package and need to ensure that spectraData() returns all core spectra variables (e.g., centroided, polarity, collisionEnergy) regardless of which ones are explicitly stored in your backend. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/missing-value-imputation-with-na) · 2 copies)
- **missing-value-iterative-imputation**: Use when an n x p data matrix contains missing values (e.g. 10-30% removed completely at random) mixing continuous and categorical variables, and non-linear relations or complex interactions are suspected. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/missing-value-iterative-imputation))
- **missing-value-knn-imputation**: Use when a metabolomics metabolite-by-sample matrix (e.g., plasma_breast_cancer.csv from Lilikoi) contains missing intensity values that must be filled before pathway transformation, feature selection, or machine learning classification. - **missing-value-pattern-simulation**: Use when augmenting MSI ion images for contrastive/self-supervised training and you need augmented views that mimic the missing-value artifacts of MS ion detection, as in DeepION's T_COL or T_ISO transforms. - **missing-value-prefiltering-before-statistical-activities**: Use when dispatching a statistical activity (t-test, U-test, ANOVA, Kruskal-Wallis) or other computation onto a chart/column in Instant Clue and the underlying dataset may contain missing values that the activity cannot handle. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/missing-value-prefiltering-before-statistical-activities))
- **missing-value-probabilistic-pca-imputation**: Use when a metabolomics abundance matrix (e.g., tomato pericarp metabolic profiles from seasons 2001, 2003, 2004) contains missing metabolite measurements that would corrupt downstream pairwise Pearson correlation computation.
